- 2026-11-24 17:53 PST 2026-11-24 18:5x PST SVC UNDRIVEN_OPEN [S81-ALLFIX-INT -> COORD, FAM-S81-SVC-OWN; cc BIND-STREAM-LAUNCH, S81-SVC-TIED] (1) Strict did not pass. The c8 strict bind FAILED with 1114 errors, 848 of them UNDRIVEN_OPEN (16 svc types x 53 groups). svc kits still launched by two routes. (a) The c8s stream writes a type record final=true as soon as that type's emit and RTL pass; analyse-phase errors (UNDRIVEN_OPEN, TIED, LF_CS) only withdraw records at bind end (bind_stream.finish). c8s was OOM-killed, so finish never ran and the svc records stayed final. BIND-STREAM-LAUNCH: a type named by an analyse-phase error must never get a final record. (b) My error: at send time my launcher upgraded every grt type to route via live lbl_gate, including the 116 types the c8 type_receipt refused for their own bind errors. Fixed now: refused types stay grt and are never upgraded. 43 refused types already run at route; their sign-off cannot count against a failing bind, so I leave them running as probes and do not count them. COORD may rule cancel. Report-only census per r703: lane UNDRIVEN-CENSUS-1124 adds a report-only UNDRIVEN_OPEN_CENSUS line to bind and posts the all-die census. (2) Cause, not a connection-map change: S81-SVC-TIED found it (note in the c9s asbuilt). Slang's empty-blackbox import reuses the first instance's port ABI, so ot_hdc_v41x_kreg (W=1..1040) and ot_hdc_v41x_dly elaborate at the wrong width. B1/c7 showed this as TIED (486,192 bits = 16 x 30,387); c8 shows the same bits as UNDRIVEN_OPEN. Fix: add both modules to slang_parameterized_blackboxes (asbuilt_run_c9s_svctied f0b6dd61e18b, already in c9s). Proof: S81-SVC-TIED's c8 + svctied probe binds (epyc9 s81-svc-tied/c8fix_out and trees/c8svctied_bind, 24 svc types) show n_undriven 0, tied 0, PASS 0 errors. (3) Nothing is tied. The 24 svc types are held out of the c8t stream and launch from the c9s bind (unit oth-ubuntu-3983120, ETA ~22:30-23:00). c8 svc kits already running will fail at gen_lint and are not relaunched on c8.
